## Honduras added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Honduras's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 files = 18 distinct works: 9 pupil textbooks (Grades 3–9, Grade 10 Matemática I and Grade 11 Matemática III BCH) and 12 teacher-guide files for Grades 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11, the Grade 8 guide held in two editions (2018 and the 2022 revision) and the Grade 1 and 2 guides each in two scans** — all from the Secretaría de Educación's PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support — and placed 335 items (218 pupil-textbook items and 117 teacher-evidence items), plus 3 duplicate-edition records (placements items 241, 242 and 338) that are cited once with their works and carry no separate items. The expansion map's “9 student + 12 teacher” count is right: the staging `books.json` files, the `phase3-push-state.json` pushed set and `textbooks.tsv` agree on exactly these 21 files. There is no Grade 1–2 pupil textbook in the Secretaría's programme (workbook only — SE Manual de Lineamientos), so the Grade 1–2 teaching in hand is the two guides; there is no Grade 3 or Grade 6 teacher guide in hand. Every file was grabbed from its recorded `source_url` (educatrachos.hn / libroshonduras.com) after the staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, and verified by exact byte size against the download-list record and by page count against the `books.json` record before reading; grabbed copies were deleted after each book's text was extracted. Read basis, stated honestly: the six text-layer pupil books and eight text-layer guides were read in full from their text; the three scanned pupil books (Grades 7–9) were read by OCR of their Índices and unit/lesson openers with interior pages OCR-sampled — Grades 7 and 8 cross-checked against the full text-layer read of their own teacher guides, which plan every lesson class by class — and the two 2022 guides are themselves scans, OCR-read at cover, presentation and index level, the Grade 9 guide therefore placed at unit grain. Counting grain: one item per Índice lesson; a lesson teaching two distinct course topics is split into two items, and three items (a Grade 4 pupil lesson, a Grade 10 pupil lesson and a Grade 2 guide unit) each carry a row in two lessons below and are counted once. The placements' named exclusions — the “Páginas para recortar” cut-out pages of the Grades 3, 4 and 5 pupil books (manipulatives, not teaching text), the Grade 6 book's closing “Repaso” review, and the “Nos divertimos” / “¿Sabías que?” enrichment boxes inside placed lessons — are stocked nowhere. The read's flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) the download list §A's original 2026-10-21 mirror labels for the III-Ciclo files were swapped between Español and Matemáticas — the files it calls “Matemáticas 7/8/9 (2018 PROMETAM)” are the Español books, and its “Español 7/8/9” alternates are the Matemáticas books — a library-record fault, already corrected in `books.json`, reported here and not fixed by this merge; (2) the Grade 9 guide's cover misprints its cycle as “II Ciclo” where the programme is III Ciclo; (3) the Grade 11 pupil book's Índice misprints “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias”, taught corrected; (4) the 2014 scan of the Grade 1 guide has a materially poorer text layer than the mirror scan of the same work, so anyone text-mining it alone will under-read it. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items — 3 pupil-textbook rows and 3 teacher-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 3er grado*), Book 2 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 4to grado*), Book 7 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 1er grado*), Book 8 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 2do grado*), Book 9 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas and 4to grado*).

The placements' named exclusions — Book A's front matter (cover, Numeracy Panel page, copyright); the Grade 7 colour-naming competency (no mathematics content as stated; the pattern competency it prepares for is counted at Grade 8); the Grade 9 “perform arithmetic operations mentally” process statement; the incomplete printed fragment “including algebraic fractions ( )” at Grade 9+; the Progress Book's non-Mathematics subject sections and its blank record apparatus; and record items R1–R3 (a Physical Education cross-reference, the front-matter GPA worked example, and the worksheets cover's external game link) — are stocked nowhere. The Progress Book's book errors are carried corrected on the rows they touch: the Grade 7 fraction list prints ½ twice (item 17); the H.C.F./L.C.M. word-problem requirement is stated a second time, misplaced inside §1.6 Decimals (item 94); three statements are truncated in the book — “involving brackets of the form”, the form never given (item 145), “a^n for n = −1, −2, −3 and rational” (item 153), and “or in the form” (item 216); §5.2 is headed “Pictograms and bar charts” but states only frequency-table work (item 151); π is printed as the Cyrillic letter “л” (item 172); and the quadratic rows are printed under the §3.5 Matrices heading with the general form garbled as “( a 2+b +c)”, read as ax² + bx + c (items 199–202). ## Myanmar added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Myanmar's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **2 documents, the Ministry of Education *Teacher Guide Mathematics-1, Grade 6* and *Teacher Guide Mathematics-2, Grade 6* (Basic Education Curriculum, Syllabus and Textbook Committee, 2019-2020; Mathematics-1: 133 PDF pages; Mathematics-2: 99 PDF pages); 0 pupil (student) textbooks** — and placed 114 teaching items. Each guide carries the full lesson plans for its half of the Grade 6 year — learning outcomes, lesson objectives, the taught subject matter with worked examples and answers, practice and review; Mathematics-1 covers the number / algebra / statistics / social-mathematics half of the year (Chapters 1–9) and Mathematics-2 the geometry / mensuration half (Chapters 1–10); the two files are two halves of one complete Grade 6 teacher-guide slot, not duplicates of each other and not parts of one file. Every Myanmar item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — the guides teach teachers how to teach the topic; neither book is a pupil textbook — cited as the evidence kind it is; Myanmar is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Myanmar pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: both guides' lesson plans follow a matching Grade 6 pupil textbook (the guides refer throughout to “the textbook”, its figures, examples and exercises — e.g. Mathematics-1 exercises 1.1–9.8); that pupil textbook exists but is not in hand (placements record item R1) — the guides' references to it are apparatus, not pupil content read here — and every Myanmar student Maths book the library records locate is an exact-link record only, never downloaded; none is part of this read and none is cited. The expansion map's count and kind split are exact (0 student + 2 teacher): textbooks.tsv carries exactly 8 Myanmar rows, all Grade 6 teacher guides, exactly two of them Mathematics; neither push-state names Myanmar (the books were secured pre-phase-3 into the textbooks repo's main tree) and phase3-staging holds no Myanmar directory, so the read copies were grabbed by the authenticated GitHub API route at the exact recorded byte sizes (Mathematics-1: 1,117,915 bytes; Mathematics-2: 1,312,022 bytes) and deleted after the read. Printed page = PDF page throughout both guides (the folio printed at the head of every page equals its PDF page). The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught content stated in a lesson plan's objective and teaching section, splitting a lesson where it teaches two contents that belong to different course lessons; the guides' shared pedagogy apparatus (student-centred method, Bloom's taxonomy, assessment, the I-T-P-R lesson stages, per-lesson introduction/practice/review conduct) is not counted per lesson, and exercise sets drawn from the matching pupil textbook are apparatus serving the lesson's own item, not separate content. The placements' named exclusions — the shared pedagogy Introduction in both guides (printed pp. 3–14 of each: student-centred method, basic rules for learning activity, Bloom's taxonomy, questioning stages, classroom assessment, 21st-century/soft skills and classroom social aspects; the syllabus tables inside it are placements record item R2 — the programme frame for the lesson plans, not separate taught content, and no syllabus-only topic was found that no lesson plan teaches), the I-T-P-R lesson-stage apparatus, entry-exit checks, group-work conduct and learning-journal directions repeated in every lesson plan, the exercise numbers cited from the pupil textbook (a book not in hand), and the glossary both prefaces announce, which is carried in the textbook and stands in neither guide as printed (recorded as an absence, verified on the read) — are stocked nowhere. The placements' record items R3–R4 (Mathematics-1's contents skips section 1.4 in its numbering although the body teaches 1.4 Distributive Property at printed pp. 26–27, and lists section 5.3 twice for the body's two successive 5.3 lessons, placed once as item 49; Mathematics-2's contents heads Chapter 9 without its chapter-number line although the body heads it “Chapter 9 Mensuration (1)” at printed p. 79) are contents slips, stocked nowhere. The placements' book errors E1–E8 are taught corrected, each noted on the row it touches. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Teacher Guide Mathematics-1, Grade 6* (teacher guide, Grade 6) — Myanmar, Ministry of Education (The Republic of the Union of Myanmar), Basic Education Curriculum, Syllabus and Textbook Committee, 2019-2020; 1,117,915 bytes, 133 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook. Covers Chapters 1–9, the number / algebra / statistics / social-mathematics half of the Grade 6 year.

## Nauru added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Nauru's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **10 documents, all of them Learning Through Doing (LTD) *Mathematics Planning Documents*, Nauru editions (2026) — Prep and Years 1–8, plus the LTD *Scope and Sequence, Years 1–8* — teacher planning alignments that sequence an Australian commercial subscription lesson system against the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus, every lesson keyed to a Nauru curriculum code (NRU-*); 0 pupil (student) textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 0 workbooks** — and placed 238 items, every one of them PLANNING/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE: the Nauru syllabus's own key-idea statements as printed on each document's closing “Nauru Curriculum Alignment” pages. Nauru publishes no government textbooks (country record, download list §§F–Q, seven fronts), so Nauru's standing in the course is the syllabus-evidence reckoning: Nauru is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count is right in number and wrong in kind: it carries Nauru as “10 student Maths books”, but every one of the 10 books.json entries is kind `planning-document` and states in its own approval note that it is NOT a Nauru government student textbook (diff fault F1 — a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge). textbooks.tsv carries 12 Nauru rows, all of them USP IOE ESD items, and zero Mathematics rows — the 10 planning documents live only in books.json and push-state. Australia overlap is ZERO: the Australia read's nine Cambridge/Jacaranda Years 8–10 pupil textbooks share no title, ISBN, byte size or file with this set, and the QCE senior titles cross-referenced in Nauru's country record are link-only, in hand for neither country and read by neither reader; nothing from the Australia placements is cited here. All 10 files were read from local disk in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records (334,327–887,725 bytes; 67 PDF pages in total; 10 distinct MD5s, no duplicates inside the set); nothing was downloaded. Printed-page basis: the documents carry no printed folios (the Scope's footer “Page N” equals its PDF page), so pages cited are document (PDF) pages — Prep key ideas on alignment pp. 6–7 of 7, Years 1–8 on alignment p. 6 of 6; the term overviews (p. 1) and weekly plans (pp. 2–5) sequence the same key ideas and add none of their own. Counting grain: one item per NRU key idea. The *Scope and Sequence* (887,725 bytes, 13 PDF pages) was read in full and cross-checked — every Scope topic traces to a year document's lessons under the codes placed — so it contributes 0 items of its own; counting it again would double-count one programme as two sources. Book errors found on the read are placed corrected, as the placements record them (diff fault F2): Year 3 NRU-M3.3 prints “suing” for “using” and NRU-G3.3 prints “jey” for “key”; Year 5 NRU-CD5.4 prints “recoding sheets” for “recording sheets”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.4 prints “co-ordiinates”; the Year 8 alignment page prints its first geometry code as “NRU-G9.1” — a Year 9 code inside the Year 8 document — while the Year 8 weekly plans cite the same lessons as NRU-G8.1, and item 226 is placed as the Year 8 item it is; the Year 2 document's Term 4 plan cites “NRU-N3.4” against Year 2 strategy-review lessons, where the alignment page's NRU-N2.4 governs; several weekly-plan code strings are garbled in print — the alignment pages' code lists are clean and govern throughout. The placements also record a document inconsistency (diff fault F3): the Scope's fractions table opens its halves entry at the earliest (Prep) level, but the Prep planning document contains no fractions anywhere — fractions enter the year documents at Year 1 (NRU-N1.8) — and the placements follow the year documents. Some key ideas are explicitly Nauru-localised (Year 3 NRU-G3.3: simple maps of Nauru; Year 6 NRU-CD6.4: data contexts “familiar to Nauruan students”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.6: graphs “using data from Nauru”). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus aims paragraph printed in every year document (general aims, no subject content); the term overviews and weekly plans as separate items (they sequence the same key ideas, and the LTD lesson titles are the Australian system's commercial catalogue, delivered by subscription — the planning documents do not reproduce the lessons, so no lesson content is placed, only the syllabus key ideas the lessons are sequenced against); “Assessment Opportunities” headers and blank assessment columns (apparatus); the Scope & Sequence's duplicated topic statements across years (one programme, counted once at key-idea grain); the 12 USP IOE ESD files filed under Nauru (not Mathematics, excluded by subject); and the OER4Pacific COL open-schooling Mathematics 11/12 PDFs named in the download list §G (regional OER, never staged under Nauru, not in hand, not read). Into this lesson the placements stock 4 planning/curriculum-evidence items from Book 1 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 2, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 2 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 3, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 3 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 4, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 4 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 5, Nauru edition (2026)*).

## Nigeria added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Nigeria’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **11 files: 1 pupil textbook and 10 teacher guides** — and placed 199 items (20 pupil-teaching items from the pupil textbook and 179 teacher-evidence items from the ten guides). The pupil textbook is *New Concept Mathematics for Senior Secondary Schools 1* (3rd ed. 2016, Learn Africa, 352 pp., 19,071,603 B), held as a release asset in `mid-size-books-8`; the guides are 7 Learn Africa guides (New Method Primary 1; New Concept JSS 1–3 and SS 1–3) and 3 Cambridge *Excellence in Mathematics* teacher’s guides (Books 1–3). The expansion map’s kind split (0 student + 10 teacher + 1 link-only) is wrong on the student count — the SS1 pupil textbook is in hand, and the map’s “1 link-only” is plausibly this same book misclassified; the true split is 1 student + 10 teacher, 11 files (flag F1, record-side). Record discrepancy (flag F2, record-side): the records describe the held JSS 1 teacher’s guide as a 2024 file of 396,871 B (4th edition), but the file in hand and read is the **2026 fifth edition** (362,534 B, ISBN 978 978 925 9755); the 2024 file is not in hand. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected (flag F3): the JSS 1 guide’s Ch20 states “1 hour = 1°” on the clock face — 1 hour = 30°; the JSS 2 guide prints “99 300 = 9.03 × 10⁴” (should be 9.93 × 10⁴), works a simple-interest example stated with T = 4 years at 3 years, and gives the Pythagorean triple “(5, 12, 23)” (should be (5, 12, 13)); the SS 1 guide prints the sector-area formula as θ/360 × 2πr² (should be θ/360 × πr²) and describes standard-form A as “between 0 and 9” (should be 1 ≤ A < 10); the SS 2 guide works log 0.000 592 and prints the characteristic without its negative/bar sign, and conflates “complementary” with “independent” events in places; the SS 3 guide calls A⁻¹ “the negative matrix” in one section (it is the inverse matrix, used correctly later), with several standard-integral lines and one index law garbled in print. The Primary 1 guide’s printed Contents page numbers do not match its body folios (flag F4) — its items cite body folios throughout. Printed-page basis: Learn Africa guide items cite the guide’s own printed (folio) pages — chapter starts from each book’s contents, verified against body folios; pupil-book items cite the pupil book’s printed pages; Cambridge items cite the guide’s printed topic-start page, each topic’s Student’s Book page span being given in the book’s scheme of work. Counting grain: one item per teaching chapter (Learn Africa books) or per teaching topic (Cambridge guides). The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: revision and exam-practice apparatus across the series (the SS1 pupil book’s Preparatory chapter and its three Revision-exercise parts; Cambridge TG1 Topics 1 and 16, TG2 Topics 1 and 18, TG3 Topics 1 and 13 and TG3’s sample examination papers); the SS 3 guide’s Ch16 “Numbers and numeration”, an explicit revision chapter; reference apparatus (the pupil book’s Tables and formulae, Four-figure tables, Answers and Index; the Cambridge guides’ Introduction sections); and the non-Maths Nigeria files checked and excluded (the English, Economics, Government, Commerce, Biology, Chemistry, Physics and Literature books in hand, the FME approved-textbooks list, and the 6 TVET trades curriculum documents — none is a Maths book). Pupil items are COUNTED normally; teacher-guide items are worded as teacher evidence and stay outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 item — 1 teacher-evidence row — from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *New Method Mathematics for Primary Schools — Teacher's Guide 1* — Nigeria, Learn Africa; 230,103 bytes, 36 PDF pp. Teacher guide — teacher evidence only. Citations are the guide's own printed (folio) pages taken from the body: the book's printed Contents page numbers do not match its body folios (the contents uses a different pagination).

|---|---|
| Money: Nigerian coins and notes, adding coin values, shopping and change within ₦50 | Nigeria — *New Method Mathematics TG 1 (Learn Africa)* (teacher guide — teacher evidence), TG printed p. 20 |

P. ## Jamaica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Jamaica's whole in-hand Maths set from the files — **8 documents: 7 National Standards Curriculum (NSC) guides that carry a Mathematics section (Grades 1, 2 and 3 Integrated Studies / Language Arts / Mathematics; Grades 4, 5 and 6 Exploratory Core; and the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide — all Ministry of Education, Youth and Information, © 2018, the Grade 6 guide in its FINAL edition of Jan 08 2020), plus 1 pupil reader** — and placed 311 items: 308 curriculum-evidence items from the guides and 3 pupil-text items from the reader. **Kind split: 0 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 7 curriculum guides.** A curriculum guide states what is REQUIRED to be taught and how to teach it; it is not a pupil textbook, so every guide item below is CURRICULUM EVIDENCE, worded “curriculum requires”, and Jamaica is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The expansion map is wrong on both count and kind (flag F1, record-side, reported not fixed): it listed Jamaica as FILES-IN-HAND with 4 student / 0 teacher Maths books — its build matched the subject field only, counting the three Grades 1–3 guides and the Grades 7–9 guide and missing the three Grades 4–6 Exploratory Core guides, whose subject field reads only “Exploratory Core” while their title pages name Mathematics as one of the four core subjects; and the four files it did count are curriculum guides, not student books. Two further record faults ride with the set (record-side; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/jamaica.md filing exists or was created): textbooks.tsv has **no row at all for the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide** (flag F2), although the file is in staging, in books.json and in the pushed set; and the TSV's Grade 5 row points at a Telegram link instead of the file's secured GitHub asset (flag F3) — the book was read byte-exact from that asset. One file defect, stated plainly: the Grade 5 guide's body font has a broken ToUnicode map, so its text layer extracts as bullet glyphs; its Mathematics section was read in full from rendered page images (120-DPI renders of PDF pp. 139–255 = printed pp. 122–238), every page legible, and it is counted as read on that basis. Printed-page basis: each guide prints its own folio; the Mathematics windows run printed = PDF − 5 (Grade 1), − 33 (Grade 2), − 16 (Grade 3), − 11 (Grade 4), − 17 (Grade 5) and − 12 (Grade 6), and Book 7's pages are its own per-grade section pages. Counting grain: Grades 1–3, one item per distinct numbered objective in the Mathematics Content Standards, plus NEW/Extended objectives and Scope-and-Sequence topics stated in no standard (marked (S&S)); Grades 4–6 and 7–9, one item per distinct objective in each Mathematics unit's objectives list; an objective restated verbatim is counted once, at its first full statement. Boundary files examined and excluded — stocked nowhere: the nursery *Birth to Three is Key* early-childhood guide (ages 0–3, all-domains; number concepts are one activity focus, with no Mathematics section or objectives at NSC grain — not a Maths book); the Grades 4–6 Resource & Technology guide (its file is a bundle whose opening leaves are the Grades 7–9 English guide; no Mathematics section); and the Civics (×2), English Language, Integrated Science and Social Studies guides (no Mathematics subject in any of them; the Science guide's measurement/graphing is science content). The placements' named exclusions inside the books are likewise stocked nowhere: the Grade 1 window's “Maths Prayers” (printed pp. 242–243 — devotional poems, religious apparatus, no mathematics content); the Scope and Sequence charts, Process Strand checklists, Illustrative Activities, Benchmarks and Illustrative Examples as apparatus (topics stated only there are placed as items and marked (S&S)); the Integrated Studies and Language Arts windows of the Grades 1–3 guides, which restate the same Mathematics attainment targets as delivery vehicles and state no requirement beyond the window's standards; the Grade 1 book's Mathematics lesson plans and “Teaching Mathematics: Ideas & Activities” (printed pp. 340–400, teacher apparatus); Book 7's Appendices A–E and closing sample lesson plan (printed pp. 191–206, activity apparatus and worked examples of the same objectives); and the reader's copyright/credits leaves. Book errors and faults are carried as the placements record them, taught corrected where they touch a row: the Grade 1 fractions standard (printed p. 275) cross-refers an objective as “Objective 2.3.27” — a Grade 2 bank number inside the Grade 1 book — and objective numbers with a leading “2.” recur throughout the Grade 1 standards (the bank numbering is shared across grades; each such objective is placed once, as stated for Grade 1); the Grade 2 addition example on printed p. 268 (“Pat has 43 oranges … How many apples Pat has now?”) mixes fruits mid-problem — a wording slip, the objective it illustrates placed as read; two Grade 2 standards pages carry a mojibake line (a Caesar-shifted “See previous page for Attainment Targets and Objectives”) — a print-production artefact, no content lost; the Grade 3 window and its Scope and Sequence state **no decimals** anywhere, so no Grade 3 decimals row is stocked; the Grade 4 Term 3 Unit 2 outcome names “cross-sections” of cubes/cuboids, which the unit nowhere teaches — placed inside its item as named-only; the Grade 4 probability unit's header prints “GRAGE 4” (sic); the Grade 5 Overview disagrees with its own units three times, recorded as printed (division of five-digit numbers sits in Term 3 Unit 1, not Term 2; the Overview's Volume has no objective anywhere in the Grade 5 Mathematics section and is stocked nowhere; lines of symmetry sit in Term 2 Unit 3, while the Term 3 Geometry unit is circles, polygons to eight sides, and pyramids); the Grade 6 Term 3 Unit 1 tyre-pressure activity is Science-linked and its arithmetic is already inside its unit's item, not placed separately; in Book 7, the Grade 9 ratio unit's footer prints “NSC Mathematics: Grade 7” (a template error — the unit sits in the Grade 9 section and is placed as Grade 9), the matrices unit header prints “GRADE 9 TERM 1: NUMBERS” although the unit sits in the Term 3 sequence (placed as Term 3), the Grade 7 constructions unit's objectives page repeats the transformations unit's first objective (counted once, in the transformations item), and the Grade 8 Term 2 geometry unit carries a header title copied from the algebra unit while its objectives are polygons and angle sums (placed by its objectives). Jamaica's pupil Maths textbooks are commercial (Carlong / Hodder / Macmillan etc.) — the library holds none; they stand in the download list only, not in hand and not read. Into this lesson the placements stock 13 curriculum-evidence rows from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 5, Book 6 and Book 7.

## Serbia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Serbia's whole in-hand Maths set from the file — **one pupil textbook**: *Математика 1* (Matematika 1 — Mathematics for the first grade of primary school), Нова школа (Nova škola), Belgrade, first edition 2018 — and placed **95 teaching items** from it. The book is a genuine, complete pupil textbook (уџбеник), not a workbook, not an exam paper and not an excerpt: every topic is presented, named and worked before it is practised. It is the only Serbia Maths book in hand — the expansion map's count and kind split (1 student, 0 teacher) are exact, and every record agrees. The file was read visually from full-page renders, every page, because its text layer is empty (a print-image PDF). It is the COMPLETE book: the CIP record gives 103 pages, the contents lists all seven units ending at printed p. 103, and the text runs printed pp. 1–103 followed by the imprint page (PDF p. 104). Printed-page basis: **printed page = PDF page** for the whole body, verified against printed folios throughout. Counting grain: one item per distinct taught skill or concept, at the point the book teaches it. The book's revision spreads (ОВО ЗНАМ / НАУЧИЛИ СМО) and its practice set (ЗАДАЦИ ЗА ВЕЖБАЊЕ) restate taught items and are named exclusions — except three places where a revision spread is the book's first presentation of new material (the number line and capacity-by-pouring on printed pp. 48–49, and balance-equivalence on printed p. 82), which are counted as teaching items and marked as such; in-lesson exercise sets are apparatus, not items. Also stocked nowhere: the front cover, contents, the seven unit divider pages and the imprint page, and the road-safety asides on printed p. 11 (no maths content). No book error was found in the book: its worked examples check out, and the contents page numbers agree with the printed folios throughout — a sheet-scale reading of printed p. 73 that seemed to omit 12 from the list of the numbers of the second ten was disproved on full-resolution re-check (the list is complete, 11, 12, 13, …, 20), an artefact of the reading scale, not a book error. Boundary, cited nowhere: Serbia's other 16 Maths files in the Kavčić free library (Grades 1–8 workbooks, textbooks and part-volumes, including the Grade 1 workbook *Математика 1 — радна свеска*) are link-only, not in hand and not read. One record-side note (reported, not fixed — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/serbia.md filing exists or was created): textbooks.tsv secures only 6 of the 7 pushed Serbia books — the Grade 4 Serbian grammar book is in books.json and in the push state but has no TSV row; it is not a maths book and does not affect this reckoning. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 rows — all from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Математика 1* (Matematika 1 — Mathematics for the first grade of primary school) (pupil textbook) — Serbia, Нова школа (Nova škola), Belgrade, first edition 2018; authors Милица Ћук, Бранислав Милошевић, Бранко Марковић; ISBN 978-86-6225-041-4; ministry approval решение 650-02-00208/2018-07 of 27. 4. 2018; 135,143,758 bytes, 104 PDF pp., MD5 9a5df48784a319517cff29b5b407d0b2. Complete book: printed pp. 1–103 + covers and imprint; printed page = PDF page throughout. Read visually from full-page renders (the file's text layer is empty). PUPIL TEXTBOOK — Serbia is counted in this lesson's taught-in figure.

## Haiti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Haiti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **28 files, every one of them teacher-side**: the nine *Programme à Compétences Minimales* (PCM) volumes for Années Fondamentales 1–9 (MENFP, Direction de l'Enseignement Fondamental, November 2019), the three IPN *Programme détaillé, Matières de base* volumes (4e, 5e, 6e année), the three *Programme Pédagogique Opérationnel* (PPO) Mathématiques fascicules for the 3e cycle (7e, 8e, 9e année, 1989-1990), the eight Nectar (MENFP) teacher sequences (four for 7e année, four for 8e année), the Nouveau Secondaire *Programme-Cadre* and the four NS detailed programmes (NS1–NS4) — every one of the 28 carrying a full Mathématiques section — and placed **1,639 items, every one of them TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE**: **Haiti has no pupil Maths textbook in hand under any label**, so Haiti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Ghana / Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count for Haiti is wrong: it carries 15, counting only the books.json entries whose subject field reads “Mathematics”, and the download list claims 43 teacher files; the true maths-bearing set is 28 files — the nine “All subjects” PCM volumes, the three IPN detailed programmes and the NS Programme-Cadre all carry full maths sections the count missed (a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge); textbooks.tsv is likewise a stale 28-row index naming only a subset of the secured files. Haiti's official pupil books (the MENFP/FAGEM *Manuels uniques* / “Mon manuel” series) are in hand for other subjects only, never for Mathematics, and the two remanié programme leads (a 7e année fascicule and a 4e année detailed programme) are not in hand — and programme documents are not textbooks under the 2026-11-10 textbook-test ruling in any case — so **Haiti stays PARTIAL for Maths, teacher evidence only**. All 28 files were read page-by-page from the secured PDFs in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records; nothing was downloaded. Read depth: the PPO 8e fascicule and the IPN 4e and 6e programmes are image-only scans and were read by OCR in full, spot-checked against the page images — where OCR garbled a worked example's digits, the placement teaches the corrected arithmetic and the case is flagged; PPO 7e PDF p. 48 and PPO 9e PDF pp. 20, 30, 31 and 32 were verified blank, separator or title pages carrying no objectives. Printed-page bases: PCM printed page = PDF page − 1; IPN 4e and 6e printed page = PDF page − 9, IPN 5e printed folios equal PDF pages; PPO 7e printed = PDF − 3, PPO 8e printed folio = PDF page, PPO 9e printed = PDF − 2; Programme-Cadre NS printed = PDF − 9 (verified against the detailed programme at §5.1–§5.2); NS1 and NS2 printed = PDF page, NS3 cites PDF pages, NS4 printed = PDF − 1; the Nectar sequences cite PDF pages by séance. The placements' eleven flags are all document-side and are carried here exactly: the PCM 6e volume's maths section header mislabels its level “5ème AF” (F1 — its items are cited to the 6e volume they stand in); PCM 5e's spoken-number row slips (“six mille deux” for 6,002, and a thousands-family slip in item 352's row) are placed corrected (F2); the PCM “orthocentre” definition error stands in the PCM **7e** volume — the diff's “(AF8)” label is the diff's own slip, disproven against the live PCM 8e section, which carries the correct circumcentre definition (F3); PCM 9e's right-asymptote and strict-inequality rows are completions of points the volume leaves silent, not errors (F4); PCM 9e's opening “années diagnostiques” ((6e), 7e, 8e) reinstate the diagnostic year the 3e-cycle fascicules dropped (F5); the PCM 7e volume announces a simple-interest objective that does not exist anywhere in its grids — nothing is placed for it, and simple interest is taught by PCM 9e and PPO 9e (F6); the Nectar “Relations entre les angles” sequence states a false corresponding-angles criterion and a mise en train with no solution, and the “Calcul littéral” sequence's séance 5 example drops a factor of x — all placed as corrected (F7, F8); the record-side faults (the map's count, the download list's count, the stale textbooks.tsv) are reported, not fixed (F9); the IPN 6e worked examples carry numerical inconsistencies (objectives 5.1, 7.1, 12.2, 16.1, 19.1, 21.1, 22.1) flagged as read and NOT asserted as print errors — the volume is an OCR-read scan and some may be digit misreads (F10); and NS2's own dating (2015 in the detailed programmes' shared headers, the school year 2008-2009 on its cover) is the book's own, not a discrepancy (F11). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the PCM volumes' other-discipline sections and their week-by-week scheduling columns beyond each distinct objective; the IPN volumes' maths avant-propos, theme indexes, other-subject sections and Suggestions/Évaluation apparatus (except where an activity is itself the taught content); the PPO fascicules' shared front matter, Programme-Cadre theme tables, progression grids, bibliographies and annexes; the Nectar sequences' summative-evaluation séances and session-management apparatus (except where a séance's activity is the taught content); the Programme-Cadre's other subjects, and the Nouveau Secondaire framework chapters shared between it and the NS1/NS2 volumes (placed once, from the Programme-Cadre volume); and the verified blank and separator pages. Into this lesson the placements stock 7 items — all teacher/curriculum-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*PCM, 2ème AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 2 (*PCM, 3ème AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 3 (*PCM, 6e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 4 (*IPN Programme détaillé, 4e année*), Book 5 (*IPN Programme détaillé, 5e année*) and Book 6 (*IPN Programme détaillé, 6e année*).

TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the ministry programme's required content for the year, not a pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page − 1.

## Suriname added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Suriname's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **18 books: 13 pupil Maths books, 3 teacher Maths books and 2 kindergarten curriculum books** (20 files examined; two further in-hand files, the LJ3 OJW *Leerlingenwerkboek* and *Ik-boek*, were checked and excluded — drawing and orientation activities with no rekenen strand and no number teaching) — and placed **376 items: 344 pupil teaching items, 7 teacher-evidence items and 25 curriculum-evidence items**. No Suriname row existed in any maths lesson before this merge, so every placed item is new here. **Set verdict, proved from the books themselves:** the expansion map's count is right for the Mathematics-labelled set (13 pupil + 3 teacher = 16 files), but its kind labels are wrong in three places: (1) the LJ6 “Leerkrachtenhandleiding Deel 1” of the records is the programme's **Algemene Inleiding** volume (Book 14), not a lesson-by-lesson guide to Deel 1; (2) the two LJ7 teacher books are **oplossingenboeken — answer keys** (Books 15–16), teacher evidence; (3) Book 4 is a **rekenboek** (lesson book), not the “Leerlingenboek” textbooks.tsv names it — the same book. **Maths was hiding under another subject label once:** the KG *Spelend leren* Themaboek 3 and 4 (Books 17–18), filed as “Play-based learning” with textbooks.tsv kind “student” — also wrong: they are **teacher curriculum books** — each carry twelve *voorbereidend rekenen* (preparatory maths) sections with stated learning goals; their 24 items are stocked as **curriculum evidence**, never pupil teaching. **Partial volumes, named as such:** Books 4 and 5 are **Deel A only** and Book 12 is **Deel 1 only**; the download list's remark that LJ8 Deel 2 is “already held” is **not borne out by any record in hand** (textbooks.tsv, the staging books.json and the push-state carry only Deel 1 for LJ8) — LJ8 Deel 2 is treated as not in hand, and the LJ4/LJ5 B-volumes and LJ8 Deel 2–3 are link-only or unrecorded, cited nowhere. Book 8 (LJ6 Deel 3) is staged and pushed but has no textbooks.tsv row — the map counted it, the TSV does not carry it. **The six flagged claims are carried exactly:** (F1) **Book 11 (LJ7 Deel 3) has no Les 24** — contents and body both jump from Les 23 (tangram) to Les 25 (tekenen op schaal); the book holds 38 lessons, not 39, and the rows cite the 38 that exist; (F2) the LJ8-Deel-2 “already held” claim, above — contradicted, not in hand; (F3) **book error, Book 6 Les 6 (printed p. 23):** the information block adds a quarter hour to 15.45 and gives the result as “06:00” — the correct result is **16:00**, and the row teaches the corrected form; (F4) **book error, Book 7 Les 14 (printed p. 54):** the information states the area of a rectangle with a garbled formula line (the perimeter formula 2l + 2b is given correctly, and the area is worked by counting in the same lesson) — the row teaches perimeter = 2l + 2b and area by counting/calculation, the forms the lesson's own worked examples use; (F5) **book error, Book 6 Les 30 (printed p. 93):** the split-subtraction check line prints minus signs where the method needs the parts added (7,986 − 5,234 split as 2,000 + 700 + 50 + 2 = 2,752) — the method itself is taught correctly in Les 21 and the rows follow the correct form; (F6) **Book 13's kind:** textbooks.tsv files the LJ10 Reader as a pupil book; its foreword states it is a guide “voor de reken-leerkracht en leerling” (for teacher and pupil) — its items are placed as teaching content, worded as a **programme reader**, not a pupil textbook. **Text-layer caveat (not a claim):** Book 3's text layer drops the digit “1” in several headings (“splitsingen van 0” for the splitting of 10); the rows use the true values, read from the sums and context. **Checked and NOT flagged:** the answer keys (Books 15–16) match their pupil books lesson for lesson (39 and 38 lessons respectively), and Book 14's contents listing of the kwartaal-1 lessons matches Book 6. Byte sizes checkable against the gov.sr records matched exactly (Books 7, 8, 10, 11). Printed-page basis: printed page = PDF page in every book except Books 17–18 (printed = PDF − 5). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the *Vooruit* books' week-closing revision pages (*weekafsluitingen*); Books 1–3's *herhaling* revision worksheets, *oefentoets* practice tests, *knipbladen* cut-out sheets and automatisation games, except where a sheet first teaches a point (those are placed); the *Plezier* books' closing games; Book 13's ch. 1 programme tables beyond item R1; and Books 17–18's non-maths theme sections. The *Plezier in rekenen* companion volumes found by the 2026-11-10 gov.sr census (LJ4 B leerlingenboek, LJ4 werkboek/takenboek, LJ5 takenboek/bijwerkboek) are link-only finds, not in hand — not read, not counted. Suriname counts in a lesson's taught-in figure only on the strength of its pupil rows; its teacher rows and curriculum rows are evidence — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent). Into this lesson the placements stock 17 items (16 pupil, 1 teacher evidence) from Book 3 (*Plezier in rekenen, Leerlingenwerkboek 3 (LJ3)*), Book 4 (*Plezier in rekenen, rekenboek A (LJ4)*), Book 5 (*Plezier in rekenen, lessenboek 5a (LJ5)*), Book 7 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 2 (LJ6)*), Book 8 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 3 (LJ6)*), Book 11 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 3 (LJ7)*), Book 13 (*Reader Rekenen/Economie (LJ10)*) and Book 16 (*Oplossingenboek Rekenen 3 (LJ7)*).

**Book 3:** *Plezier in rekenen — Leerlingenwerkboek 3, Leerjaar 3* (pupil workbook) — Suriname, MinOV, 2018; 4,766,969 bytes, 60 PDF pp. Pupil workbook. Printed page = PDF page. (Text-layer caveat: this file's text layer drops the digit “1” in several headings — “splitsingen van 0” is the splitting of 10; the rows cite the true values, read from the sums and context.)

**Book 4:** *Plezier in rekenen — rekenboek A, Leerjaar 4* (pupil book) — Suriname, Minov, 2013 (the Surinamese version of *De Wereld in Getallen*); 17,741,213 bytes, 69 PDF pp. Pupil book. Printed page = PDF page. **Deel A only** — no Deel B in hand. (textbooks.tsv names it “Leerlingenboek”; it is a lesson book (rekenboek) — the same book.)

**Book 5:** *Plezier in rekenen — lessenboek 5a, Leerjaar 5* (pupil book) — Suriname, Minov, 2014; 10,033,668 bytes, 67 PDF pp. Pupil book. Printed page = PDF page. **Deel A only** — no Deel B in hand.

**Book 7:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 2, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 6* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2023; 5,713,419 bytes, 144 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page (byte size matches the gov.sr record exactly). Kwartaal 2, lessons 1–39.

**Book 8:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 3, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 6* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2023; 2,709,444 bytes, 136 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page (byte size matches the gov.sr record exactly). Kwartaal 3, lessons 1–39. (The book is staged and pushed but carries no textbooks.tsv row — a record gap, reported, not fixed by this merge.)

**Book 11:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 3, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 7* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2022; 20,440,292 bytes, 120 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page (byte size matches the gov.sr record exactly). Kwartaal 3 — **38 lessons (1–23, 25–39): there is no Les 24 in this book**; the numbering jumps 23 → 25 in contents and body alike.

**Book 13:** *Reader Rekenen/Economie, Leerjaar 10* (programme reader) — Suriname, MinOWC, November 2022; 1,198,264 bytes, 31 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. A programme reader: learning outcomes, theory summaries and exercises **for teacher and pupil** (its foreword calls it a guide “voor de reken-leerkracht en leerling”); textbooks.tsv files it as a pupil book — its rows here are worded as a programme reader, not a pupil textbook, and its teaching items are carried as the foreword states them.

**Book 16:** *Vooruit met Rekenen 3 — Oplossingenboek, Leerjaar 7* (answer key) — Suriname, MinOWC; 211,894 bytes, 48 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — worked answers to the lessons of Book 11 (38 lessons; there is no Les 24). Printed page = PDF page.

## Papua New Guinea added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Papua New Guinea’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **32 files = 28 works, all Papua New Guinea Department of Education (NDoE), “Issued free to schools by the Department of Education”: 8 pupil textbook files (7 works — the JICA/QUIS-ME National Mathematics Textbooks, Grades 1–6, with Grades 3 and 5 in two parts each) and 24 teacher files (21 works — 9 JICA teacher’s manual files, 6 works with Grades 3–5 in two parts each, and 15 Standards Based Curriculum (SBC) teacher guides, Elementary Prep to Grade 12, with separate Advanced and General guides at Grades 11 and 12)** — and placed 503 items (102 from the pupil textbooks, 401 teacher-evidence items from the manuals and guides). The expansion map is wrong on both of its numbers (flag 1 — record-side, reported, not fixed by this merge): it said 6 student + 22 teacher in hand; the true set is 8 student + 24 teacher. The map missed three files that exist only as textbooks.tsv rows (the Grade 4 and Grade 6 full-year JICA pupil textbooks and the Grade 6 JICA manual) and did not know the Grade 2 SBC guide, which has a staging books.json row but no textbooks.tsv row and no push-state entry (flag 4). **Filing error (flag 2 — record-side): the git-tree copies recorded for the Grade 1 and Grade 2 JICA manuals are a different country’s book** — both TSV tree paths hold the identical file, a Mozambique Grade 1 teacher manual in Portuguese (“Manual do Professor, 1.ª classe”, 5,891,019 B / 216 pp, the same MD5 at both paths — the only byte-identical duplicate pair found in this hunt, and not a PNG book), which also sits in the release `mid-size-books-8` correctly named as a Mozambique book. The genuine PNG manuals were read from the GitHub release `full-quality-oversized-books`, whose assets match the staged sizes and checklist MD5s exactly; the push-state’s per-folder landing claims for those two files should be treated as unreliable until the tree paths are re-filed. Flag 3: the two manuals’ recorded education.gov.pg source URLs do not serve the staged pressings — both returned an HTML page during the read, and a later re-probe served a different pressing of the Grade 1 manual — so the staged pressings cannot be re-grabbed from the recorded source URLs. Flag 5: the Grade 9 SBC guide’s textbooks.tsv tree path 404s; the file was recovered by searching all release assets and matches books.json on size and pages. No Maths hides under other subject labels in the PNG records — all 156 staging books.json entries and all 163 textbooks.tsv rows were checked by subject, title and path. Link-only Maths not in hand and not read (it does not count against closure): the FODE distance course-books for Grades 7–12 Mathematics / Maths A / Maths G (unit PDFs) and the two OBE upper-secondary guides found 2026-11-10 (Advanced Mathematics TG, ISBN 978-9980-9924-8-2; General Mathematics TG). Book errors found on the read are taught corrected: the Grade 1 Textbook and its manual print “Subtracation” in the Chapter/Unit 12 title; the SBC guides print “Angels” for Angles in places, “Algebrain” for Algebraic (a Grade 11 guide’s Factorisation unit header) and “Orobability” for Probability (the Grade 9 guide’s strand table); the Grade 1 SBC guide’s Term-1 record prints “tenths” where tens is meant. The Grade 3 SBC programme has no Money topic where the JICA Grade 3 textbook carries one — a programme difference, not an error. Printed-page basis: JICA textbook pages cite the book’s own printed folio / contents page; JICA manuals cite the manual’s own printed contents page; SBC guides cite the guide’s printed programme/contents pages (the basis is stated in each book entry below); where a contents extraction was partly two-column-interleaved, the PDF page is given as well and marked “PDF p.”. Counting grain: one item per chapter (pupil textbooks), per unit (teacher’s manuals — the manual unit mirrors the textbook chapter of the same number and is counted once, as teacher evidence), and per named topic/unit in the SBC guides’ own programmes. The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the end-of-book/year “Summary” chapters and units in the JICA textbooks and manuals (review apparatus), and the SBC guides’ front sections, assessment-record templates, glossaries/resources and integrated cross-subject project sections. Pupil-textbook rows are COUNTED normally; every manual and SBC guide row is teacher evidence — named in the countries line and in the rows, but outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items — 1 pupil-textbook row and 3 teacher-evidence rows — from Book S4 (*National Mathematics Textbook Grade 3, Part 2*), Book T4 (*National Mathematics Teacher's Manual Grade 3, Part 2*), Book T10 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary Prep (SBC)*), Book T11 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary One (SBC)*).

**Book S4:** *National Mathematics Textbook Grade 3, Part 2* (pupil textbook) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME), 2019; 14,426,183 bytes, 110 PDF pp. PUPIL TEXTBOOK — counted. Part 2 = Chapters 11–20 (opens at Chapter 11, Large Numbers).

**Book T4:** *National Mathematics Teacher’s Manual Grade 3, Part 2* (teacher’s manual) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME); 14,847,377 bytes, 170 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — as Book T3. Part 2 = Units 11–20.

**Book T10:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary Prep* (SBC teacher guide) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum, 2015; 6,660,506 bytes, 206 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — scripted daily lesson plans (printed pp. 23–184); the topics stocked here are the guide’s own, from its Planning and Programming tables (printed pp. 8–22) and its Assessment strand tables (printed pp. 185–187). No SBC pupil textbook is in hand at any grade.

**Book T11:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary One* (SBC teacher guide) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum, 2015; 8,102,294 bytes, 201 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — scripted daily lesson plans; the topic list is the guide’s own, from its Assessment plan (printed pp. 182–183) and term assessment records. Book error noted on the read: the Term-1 record prints “tenths” where tens is meant in the place-value breakdown of 24.

| Money — exchanging coins for notes | Papua New Guinea — *Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary One (SBC)* (SBC teacher guide — teacher evidence), the guide’s Assessment plan, printed pp. 182–183 |

Papua New Guinea is added to the countries line and to the acceptance count above on its pupil rows — 1 pupil item in this lesson, from real pupil textbooks of Papua New Guinea (the JICA/QUIS-ME National Mathematics Textbooks); its 3 teacher-evidence rows in this lesson (JICA teacher’s manuals and SBC teacher guides) are teacher evidence only, never the basis of the count. Papua New Guinea’s class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Papua New Guinea Maths book (all 32 files) has been read in full and stocked.

## Sweden added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Sweden's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 files, 594 PDF pages**, of which **10 carry mathematics**: the national curriculum Lgr22, 2 SPSM pupil materials, and 7 SPSM teacher guides, notes and support publications. Sweden has **no state pupil textbook series** — textbook approval was abolished in 1991, Skolverket sets the curriculum (Lgr22), and the state producer SPSM (Specialpedagogiska skolmyndigheten) publishes special-needs materials — so the pupil teaching in hand is SPSM material for blind and deaf pupils, and nothing else: 13 pupil items (the Abakus exercise book and the Växelspelet money game), 81 teacher-evidence items and 79 curriculum-evidence items from Lgr22's Matematik kursplan, 173 items in all. The other 3 files in the read set prove on reading to carry no maths at all and are stocked nowhere: *Skrivmaterial för elever som behöver förstärkta linjer och rutor* is blank ruled/grid stationery (copying masters), the *ABC Skriva-Läsa* teacher guide is literacy only, and a generically named *Arbetsblad* worksheet (pharmacy vocabulary) was pulled in as a hidden-maths spot check and cleared negative. The expansion map is wrong on both axes (flag F4): it listed 8 files as 3 student + 5 teacher, but two of its eight are not Maths at all (F1: the Skriva-Läsa guide, filed in books.json under “Matematik; Svenska”, is a literacy guide — its only number-adjacent moment, drawing a numbered slip 2–5 to pick that many pictures (printed p. 6), is classroom apparatus; F3: the Skrivmaterial, counted as a student Maths book, is blank stationery), a third is the wrong kind (F2: *Räkna med mig*, filed as a student book, is a 105-page handbook for teachers of braille-using pupils — all 35 of its items are teacher evidence), and four maths-bearing files were missed by the map entirely — Lgr22 itself (its section 5.5 is the full Matematik syllabus), the *Arbeta med språkstörning i matematikundervisningen* support publication (filed under generic special education), *Växelspelet* (filed under “Verklighetsuppfattning”) and the *Jag handlar* teacher guide (filed under home-economics subjects). Flags F1–F4 are record/map-side: they are reported here and carried in this section; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/sweden.md filing exists. The books' own errors are carried corrected: F5 — both Abakus volumes misprint their print ISBNs with a “987-” prefix (Övningsbok “987-91-28-11602-2”, Handledning “987-91-28-11600-8”; each book's PDF ISBN carries the correct 978- prefix); F6 — *Räkna med mig*'s ISBN is printed malformed as “98-91-28-10386-2”; F7 — the Abakus Handledning's division chapter (printed p. 45) says the pupil must master “multiplikation” where the sense requires division, taught corrected on the division row it touches; F8 — a box in *Räkna med mig* (printed p. 38) misnames the Abakus handbook as “Räkna med mig – handledning”. The placements' record items are stocked nowhere: R1 (Lgr22's assessment criteria, printed pp. 60–63 — they restate the central content as standards and add no content beyond the items), R2 (the Abakus Handledning's exercise sets and maths-card copying masters), R3 (the Arbeta publication's overview material), R4 (*Räkna med mig*'s apparatus), R5 (the Diamanterna card game itself is a physical product, not a book, and is not in hand — only its one-page SPSM note is) and R6 (the *Jag handlar* pupil book is recorded only as a sample that was never staged as a secured file; only its teacher guide is in hand). One basis note: the Övningsbok's multiplication and division exercise pages are abacus diagrams with no printed numerals, so those two placements rest on the book's contents page, section headings and introduction, exactly as the placements state. Sweden's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-heavy)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but 160 of its 173 items are teacher or curriculum evidence and its pupil side is special-needs material only. Into this lesson the placements stock 5 items: 3 teacher evidence, 2 pupil teaching. Sweden's pupil items in this lesson are genuine pupil teaching, so Sweden is added to this lesson's acceptance count above and to the countries line; its teacher and curriculum rows here are evidence only and carry no count.

**Book 3:** *Växelspelet* (pupil game rules) — Sweden, SPSM, art. 12800; 1,593,356 bytes, 4 PDF pp., MD5 09c5d0cdd084e777ab56394dd4c49ed4. A pupil game about exchanging Swedish money, cited by its printed pp. 1–4; filed in the records under “Verklighetsuppfattning”, not under Matematik — confirmed on reading as a pupil maths game. PUPIL TEACHING.

**Book 8:** *Lärarhandledning till Jag handlar* (teacher guide) — Sweden, SPSM; 9,514,421 bytes, 4 PDF pp., MD5 25da43f7cd1bb5040f69525dd63cfcee. Guide to the 11-chapter pupil shopping book *Jag handlar* (pupil book pp. 4–76+); grabbed from its recorded source_url after the GitHub Contents API returned 404. The pupil book itself is NOT in hand — the library records a sample (smakprov) of it that was never staged as a secured file (record item R6) — so every item from this guide is teacher evidence taken from the guide, and the money pages are the guide's per-chapter “Vad kostar det?” pages. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 10:** *Räkna med mig* (teacher handbook) — Sweden, SPSM, 2018, best.nr 10386; 20,465,920 bytes, 105 PDF pp., MD5 5fbeac506f239001f466a59aa7574d9e. A 105-page handbook on mathematics teaching for compulsory-school pupils who use braille, addressed to teachers (foreword, printed p. 7) and built on the Lgr11 central content; grabbed from its recorded source_url after the GitHub Contents API returned 404. Filed in the records as a student book — wrong (flag F2): it is not a pupil book, and all 35 of its items are teacher evidence. Book errors: its ISBN is printed malformed as “98-91-28-10386-2” (flag F6), and a box on printed p. 38 misnames the Abakus handbook as “Räkna med mig – handledning” (flag F8); the Abakus guide placed from Book 4 is the real book. Its apparatus — the accessible-learning-environment and adapted/national tests chapters (printed pp. 14–26), the tools and materials catalogue (pp. 85–91), the maths word list (p. 95), the table of common signs in maths braille (pp. 96–97) and the copying masters (pp. 98–103) — is record item R4, stocked nowhere. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

|---|---|
| Counting 1, 2, 5 and 10 kr coins and writing the sum; the guide caps every sum at 35 kr (Jag handlar guide) | Sweden — *Lärarhandledning till Jag handlar* (teacher guide) — teacher evidence only (the guide, 4 PDF pp.) |
| Choosing which sum of money pays for a given item (Jag handlar guide) | Sweden — *Lärarhandledning till Jag handlar* (teacher guide) — teacher evidence only (the guide, 4 PDF pp.) |
| The Swedish denominations the game uses: 1 kr coins; 2, 5 and 10 kr; notes of 20, 50, 100, 200, 500 and 1000 kr | Sweden — *Växelspelet* (pupil game rules) — pupil teaching, printed pp. 1–4 |
| Exchanging money across denominations — the game's core question “Kan du växla?” (“Can you exchange?”) | Sweden — *Växelspelet* (pupil game rules) — pupil teaching, printed pp. 1–4 |
| Adapted money addition: adding the prices 48+59+67+5+5 kr; “which two things do you buy — you pay 53 / 74 / 67 / 57 kr” (tactile läromedel) | Sweden — *Räkna med mig* (teacher handbook) — teacher evidence only, printed p. 30 |

Sweden enters this lesson as pupil teaching on the rows marked pupil teaching above — the Övningsbok and Växelspelet are genuine pupil materials (SPSM special-needs publications) — and is counted in the acceptance count above on those pupil rows alone; every other Sweden row in this lesson is teacher or curriculum evidence and is counted nowhere. Sweden's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-heavy): the in-hand set is read and stocked in full, but there is no mainstream pupil textbook in hand — Sweden has no state pupil textbook series, and its pupil side here is SPSM material for blind and deaf pupils only.

## Switzerland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Switzerland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **8 files / 6 works, and no pupil textbook among them**: the three PER brochures *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales*, Cycles 1, 2 and 3 (CIIP, Version 3.0, août 2024), whose Mathématiques parts are placed here; the Ticino *Piano di studio della scuola dell'obbligo ticinese* (DECS, edizione perfezionata 2022), whose Matematica chapter is placed here; the *Lehrplan 21 Graubünden* (Fassung vom 15.03.2016), whose Mathematik plan is placed here — one work in three filed language editions (German, Romansh, Italian), read in full in the German edition, verified identical in structure and pagination in the other two, and cited once; and one teacher guide, *MaMa — Linee guida del progetto MaMa: Ambito Numeri e calcolo* (DECS/CeRDD, 2021), whose didactic rulings are placed here as teacher evidence — 425 items in all (364 curriculum evidence + 61 teacher-guide evidence, 0 pupil). The expansion map's kind split is WRONG (record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records): it recorded "3 student + 1 teacher", but the three "student" files are the PER **curriculum** brochures mislabelled — **Switzerland has 0 pupil Maths textbooks in hand**, and its prescribed pupil series (Schweizer Zahlenbuch, Mathematik Primarstufe / Sekundarstufe I (LMVZ), Mathwelt, Mathbuch, MER Mathématiques 1–11, Ticino's Base Matematica 1–4) are commercial and licence-gated, not in hand and not read. Further maths was found hiding under "all subjects" labels: the Ticino Piano and the Lehrplan 21 Graubünden are all-subjects volumes carrying full mathematics chapters. Every grabbed file was verified SHA-256 byte-exact against its books.json record. Checked in hand and excluded with reasons, stocked nowhere: the three PER *Aperçus des contenus* (one example-grain maths page each — record items R1–R3), the PER *Présentation générale* (framework structure only), the PER *Éducation numérique* brochure (the computer-science curriculum; mathematics appears only as cross-links — a separate school subject) and the four Vaud *<DÉ>CODAGE* digital-education teacher manuals (contents pages of all four read: Science informatique / Médias / Usages only — a separate school subject); inside the PER brochures, the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts are other subjects, the *Modélisation* objectives are process apparatus whose content is carried by the per-theme problem-solving items, and the Lexiques are apparatus; inside the Ticino volume, every chapter other than Matematica is another subject and the process/attitude competence targets carry no subject content; inside the Lehrplan 21 volumes, every subject other than Mathematik is out of scope and the Einführung/Gesamtüberblick chapters are structure, not content; MaMa §§1 and 3 are project apparatus, and §2's quoted Ticino targets are placed at the Ticino items, not double-counted (record item R4). One within-document inconsistency is flagged so it is not stocked: the PER Cycle 1 *Aperçus des contenus* claims Cycle 1 pupils "calculate lengths (perimeter of a rectangle), areas, volumes (cube)" — the governing Cycle 1 brochure requires only comparison and measurement with an étalon (items 46–55), and perimeter/area/volume calculation begins in Cycle 2 (items 158–160); the brochure governs, the Aperçu's Cycle 1 sentence is an overstatement, and it is NOT stocked as a Cycle 1 requirement (record item R1). One document error is called out by a source itself and taught corrected: the MaMa guide rules that there is no "dissociative property" of addition — splitting an addend and associating (16 + 19 = 16 + 10 + 9) is the associative property, and textbooks that name it otherwise are wrong (item 378); the guide's other flagged classroom errors are misconceptions it warns against, not errors it commits. No Liechtenstein file is judged here: Liechtenstein teaches from Switzerland's books, but no file is filed under Liechtenstein in any record. Printed-page basis: PER brochures, Ticino Piano and Lehrplan 21 — printed folio = PDF page − 2; MaMa — printed page = PDF page. Switzerland's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but all 425 of its items are curriculum or teacher-guide evidence and no pupil textbook is in hand. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 item (1 curriculum evidence) from Book 6. Switzerland is added to the countries line and named in the rows below as evidence only, and is NOT added to the acceptance count, on the standing evidence-only precedent.

**Book 6:** *Lehrplan 21 Graubünden — Gesamtausgabe* (cantonal curriculum) — Switzerland, Erziehungs-, Kultur- und Umweltschutzdepartement Graubünden, Fassung vom 15.03.2016; German edition 60,184,274 bytes, 494 PDF pp.; SHA-256 verified exact against its books.json record. Printed folio = PDF p. − 2. ONE work in three filed language editions — German (read in full), Romansh *Plan d'instrucziun 21* (57,219,009 bytes, 488 PDF pp.) and Italian *Piano di studio 21 Grigioni* (56,644,563 bytes, 492 PDF pp.), both verified as the same work in translation (identical structure, pagination and MA competency codes) — and cited once. Only the Mathematik chapter (printed pp. 194–221) is placed here; every other subject in the volume is out of scope. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the plan states the competencies pupils must reach and teaches nothing itself; its rows are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| MA.3.A.1 — Lehrplan 21 requires pupils to understand and use terms and symbols for quantities, functions, data and chance: money in Fr./Rp., interest, rebate, gross/net and profit (cycle 3), probability and factorial notation | Switzerland — *Lehrplan 21 Graubünden* (cantonal curriculum) — curriculum evidence only, MA.3 (Grössen, Funktionen, Daten und Zufall), printed pp. 215–221 |

Switzerland enters this lesson as evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count — Switzerland has no pupil Maths textbook in hand; its in-hand set is three curriculum brochures, the mathematics chapters of two all-subjects curriculum volumes and one teacher guide, which state requirements and teaching guidance without being pupil textbooks (the standing evidence-only precedent). Switzerland's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): the whole in-hand set is read and stocked, and it advances only when a Swiss pupil Maths textbook — one of the prescribed commercial series, today licence-gated — lands in hand and is read.

## Timor-Leste added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Timor-Leste's whole in-hand library from the PDFs — **5 files**, of which **2 carry mathematics**: the Grade 5 pupil exercise book *Livru Ezersísiu Matemátika 5* and the Matemátika section (printed pp. 318–511) of *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 3 Períodu 2*, the combined all-subject Grade 3 Period-2 lesson-plan volume. The other three files were checked in full and carry no maths teaching: *Livru Dalan Tetun ba Klase 5* (a Tetum grammar book — number words appear once, as one of eight grammatical word-classes), *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 6 Períodu 2* (Natural and Social Science plans only; its four “matemátika” mentions sit inside one density lesson) and *Um Mundo de Histórias 6º ano* (a Portuguese reader; maths appears only inside stories). The expansion map's “1 student + 0 teacher” split counts only the books filed as Maths and repeats a filing error — **flag F1** (record-side: reported here, not fixed; merges never edit the records, and no `maths/books/timor-leste.md` filing exists): the Grade 3 volume is filed as the “Ciências Naturais Grade 3 Teacher Guide”, claiming Natural Science plans from p. 10 and Social Science plans from p. 468, but its own Índise (printed p. 3) lists all nine basic-education components and puts Matemátika at p. 318, Siénsia Naturál at p. 512 and Siénsia Sosiál at p. 624 — the mis-filing hid the country's only in-hand Maths teacher evidence. **Flag F2** (record gap): `textbooks.tsv` carries only 3 Timor-Leste rows — the two Grade 5 books, including the Maths exercise book, sit in `books.json`, the push state and the GitHub repo tree with no TSV row. Everything else Timor-Leste Maths is link-only and not in hand — the Matemática G1–G4 student books, the G10–G12 student manuals, the 2024 new-curriculum Grade 7 set and the Learning Passport book courses — and is cited nowhere. The placements number **177 items: 83 pupil-workbook items and 94 teacher-evidence items** (33 from full printed lesson plans; 61 from the period plan table alone, each marked “(plan table only)”). The workbook is a genuine pupil teaching book — 77 numbered exercise sets (Ezersísiu 1–77) plus two front-matter reading tables — but it practises and tests; it does not present topics first (its letter to teachers states the exercises follow the annual plan and lesson-plan sequence), and it is not the main Grade 5 textbook. The guide's Matemátika section is **Period 2 only** — the Period 1 and Period 3 volumes are not in hand, so Grade 3 coverage here is one period of three. The books' own errors are taught corrected: **E1** — the workbook's census table heads its second column “2005”, but its figures are the 2010 census and its questions ask about 2010; **E2** — Ezersísiu 70 is a reprint of Ezersísiu 68, counted once (as item 70); **E3** — Ezersísiu 60's fourth prism has equal dimensions in the Tetum text and different dimensions in the Portuguese (the Tetum governs); **E4** — the guide's Lisaun 51 plan tells the teacher to write “6 x 1 = 1” (read 6 x 1 = 6, as the page's own explanation gives); **E5** — the guide's introduction states 2 formal formative assessments per period while the plan schedules 6 (assessments are exclusions either way); **E6** — ten blank plan-table content cells are continuation/test rows, folded into their parent items, and lesson 84's body settles it as the fraction–decimal relation lesson. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the workbook's covers, imprint, forewords, letters and “do not write in this book” notice; the guide's discipline introduction and assessment guidance (printed pp. 326–328); its 22 consolidation lessons, 6 formal-assessment lessons and 5 project lessons (no new content stated for any of them); blank continuation/test rows; homework and in-lesson revision steps; and the Grade 3 volume's other eight subject sections. Counting grain: pupil book — one item per distinct practised skill; teacher guide — one item per distinct taught content in the period plan. Timor-Leste's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — a pupil-workbook set on the Peru pattern: the workbook's rows count, the guide's rows are teacher evidence and count nowhere. Into this lesson the placements stock 5 items: 1 pupil-workbook + 4 teacher evidence.

**Book 1:** *Livru Ezersísiu Matemátika 5* (pupil workbook — the Grade 5 Mathematics exercise book) — Ministério da Educação de Timor-Leste; bilingual Tetum/Portuguese; based on the 2014 National Basic Education Curriculum (Cycles 1–2), 1st edition May 2016, print run 38,900; 1,997,219 bytes, 70 imposed sheets (140 half-pages), MD5 44cd93b05692baaa17cc58569dc9cc5b. Complete as filed: Ezersísiu 1–77 all present between the front cover/imprint/forewords block and the back cover. **No folio is printed anywhere in the book** — the PDF is a printer's imposition, two portrait book pages per landscape sheet — so its items are cited by exercise number (Ezersísiu N). PUPIL WORKBOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure (the Peru workbook pattern).

**Book 2:** *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 3 Períodu 2* (teacher guide — the combined all-subject Grade 3 Period-2 lesson-plan volume; its Matemátika section only) — Ministério da Educação de Timor-Leste; 15,881,272 bytes, 829 PDF pp., MD5 18b0a4e0d55af5f56868c556dc9893f2. Its Índise (printed p. 3) lists all nine basic-education components; the Matemátika section (printed pp. 318–511) was read in full — full 50-minute lesson plans (objective, materials, timed teaching steps; the MAT3.x codes are the national curriculum's Grade 3 outcomes) plus the period plan table at printed pp. 318–325. Printed page = PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — its rows are named in a lesson but counted in no taught-in figure, and the section is Period 2 only. Filed in the library records as the “Ciências Naturais Grade 3 Teacher Guide” — the filing error F1 carried in the intro above.

## Uganda added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Uganda's whole in-hand Maths library from the PDFs — **8 files / 8 works: 7 pupil books + 1 teacher's guide**, all Uganda Ministry of Education and Sports / NCDC *Home-Study Learning* books (August 2020) — and the expansion map's “7 student + 1 teacher, FILES-IN-HAND” count and split are **correct**, with one correction of kind: the Primary Five pupil book is not a standalone Maths book — it is the Mathematics booklet (package PDF pp. 9–166) inside the NCDC Primary Five merged home-study package (Mathematics + Integrated Science + English + Social Studies, 350 pp.). Maths content hiding under an all-subjects label was checked for across the whole in-hand set; this package is the only such volume. The seven pupil books (Primary Four, the Primary Five booklet, Primary Six, Senior One, Senior Two, Senior Three, Senior Four) are real pupil teaching books — lessons, worked examples and exercises addressed to the learner — so Uganda's pupil rows **count** in a lesson's taught-in figure. The one teacher book, the Senior One *Teacher's Guide* (prototype, 76 pp.), is **teacher evidence only** and counts nowhere; it covers all 12 Senior One prototype topics, including five topics (1 Number Bases, 2 Working with Integers, 8 Angle Properties, 9 Data Collection and Presentation, 12 Time and Time Tables) for which no Senior One pupil text is in hand, and its evidence for those topics carries that “no pupil book in hand” caveat. NOT in hand, and cited nowhere: the Senior One prototype pupil Mathematics textbook (NCDC posted only the Teacher's Guide) and the Scribd combined S1&S2 Science Package (no download route). The placements number **324 items: 278 pupil items and 46 teacher-evidence items (TG1–TG46)** — one item per distinct lesson in the home-study books, one per distinct topic treatment in the guide. Page citations are PDF pages for the pupil books (in the primary home-study books the printed folios run 6 behind: printed = PDF − 6; Primary Five pages are the merged package's PDF pages) and the guide's printed pages for the TG items. The books' own errors are taught corrected: the Senior One home-study book heads the conversion example at PDF p. 8 “Convert 13/4 into an improper fraction” — 13/4 is already improper, and the worked example in fact converts it to the mixed number 3¼; the Primary Six book's triangle-angles exercise (PDF p. 85) prints two interior angles as “360 and 540” — the degree signs set as zeros, read as 36° and 54°; the Senior Three book heads its mensuration topic “TRIGONOMETRY” while its own stated outcomes and lessons are units, surface area and volume — taught as mensuration. Uganda's own ways are kept: equations written with a shape for the unknown before letters (Primary Four); integers walked on a ground number line, face forward for positive and back for backward (Primary Five and Six); base five taught in primary school (Primary Five); fractions taught by slicing chapattis (Senior One); income tax worked from Uganda's own tax bands and allowances (Senior Three). Uganda's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every Maths book in hand has been read fresh and placed; what remains outside the set (the Senior One prototype pupil textbook, the Senior Two–Four prototype books and guides) is not in hand and is a library hunting matter, not a course hold. Into this lesson the placements stock 5 pupil items.

**Book 1:** *Mathematics Home-Study Learning, Primary Four* (pupil book) — Uganda, Ministry of Education and Sports / National Curriculum Development Centre (NCDC), August 2020; 3,968,012 bytes, 74 PDF pp., MD5 72ec063ca3c0ecb939a2c5e9a63a2412. Cover (“HOME-STUDY LEARNING / PRIMARY 4 / MATHEMATICS / August 2020”) and NCDC colophon read directly. Pages are cited by PDF page; the printed folios run 6 behind (printed = PDF − 6). PUPIL BOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 2:** *Home-Study Learning, Primary Five* — the Mathematics booklet only (pupil book) — Uganda, Ministry of Education and Sports / NCDC, August 2020; the Primary Five book is not a standalone Maths book: it is the Mathematics booklet (package PDF pp. 9–166, 158 pp.) inside the merged home-study package (Mathematics + Integrated Science + English + Social Studies); the package file is 10,444,968 bytes, 350 PDF pp., MD5 5159920c66cfd1869ff19fd9cf119fea, and the booklet's interior is verified as the Primary Five Mathematics home-study book. Pages are cited by the package's PDF page. PUPIL BOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

Cover and NCDC colophon read directly. Pages are cited by PDF page; the printed folios run 6 behind (printed = PDF − 6). PUPIL BOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.
